<!-- Provenance: captured 20 August 2026 by an agent driving the rendered
arena.ai page, one board at a time, no API. Style Control was confirmed ON for
every board in the same page read, two ways: the ranking-style-control control
reading aria-checked="true", and the rendered footer printing "Default
Leaderboard Plots". That footer marker reads backwards, and it is the reason an
earlier set of figures had to be withdrawn, so it is checked and not assumed.

Category selection was done by dispatching a click on the real DOM button found
by its visible text, because a rotating sidebar advert shifted the layout
between screenshot and click and made pixel targeting unreliable. Still a real
interaction with the rendered page, not an API call.

Verified against the owner's own Style Control capture: 30 of 30 overlapping
Overall values match exactly.

Occupational is absent because it is not a board. Clicking it only expands a
submenu of eight occupation subcategories; there is no aggregate to read.

None of these boards is scored on the picker. The four Arena figures it does
score are Hard Prompts, Creative Writing, Instruction Following and Longer
Query, which are held elsewhere. -->

# LM Arena Text Leaderboard — Category Boards, Style Control ON

Captured 20 August 2026 from the live rendered page at arena.ai (Text Arena).
Every board below had the Style Control adjustment toggled ON, verified in the
same page read by confirming both the checked radio state of the Style
Control control and the footer plot label reading "Default Leaderboard
Plots" (not "Remove Style Control Leaderboard Plots"). Full ranked lists were
expanded via "View all" before transcription. All figures are transcribed
verbatim from the page; none are estimated or rounded.
